# Homeric word = paraphrase word(s) that render it, per line.
# -        no counterpart in the paraphrase
# <w / >w  w is in the previous / next line's paraphrase (>>w: two lines on)
# w@2      the 2nd occurrence of w in that paraphrase line (default: first not yet used)
# Paraphrase words not listed are Gaza's additions.
